"""Each boundary function's fake keeps its contract signature and serves its fixtures."""

import inspect
import json
from dataclasses import asdict, replace
from pathlib import Path

import contract_support as support
import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch

from rllab import baselines, charts, core, evaluate, methods, runlog, train, worlds
from rllab.core import Box
from rllab.methods import SpecMismatchError
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

FAKES = {
    "core.play_episode": core.play_episode,
    "worlds.make": worlds.make,
    "baselines.rule_for": baselines.rule_for,
    "baselines.baseline_means": baselines.baseline_means,
    "methods.make_learner": methods.make_learner,
    "methods.load_policy": methods.load_policy,
    "train.load_run_configs": train.load_run_configs,
    "train.run_check": train.run_check,
    "train.run_train": train.run_train,
    "train.run_batch": train.run_batch,
    "runlog.find_runs": runlog.find_runs,
    "runlog.open_run": runlog.open_run,
    "runlog.progress": runlog.progress,
    "charts.learning_curve": charts.learning_curve,
    "evaluate.final_evaluation": evaluate.final_evaluation,
}


def fake(folder):
    return getattr(support.load(folder, "fake"), folder.split(".")[1])


def snake_spec():
    return support.world_spec(support.read("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default"))


def snake_config():
    return support.run_config(support.read("train.RunConfig", "snake"))


def as_json(value):
    return json.loads(json.dumps(asdict(value), default=str))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("folder", sorted(FAKES))
def test_fake_has_the_contract_signature(folder):
    real = inspect.signature(FAKES[folder])
    assert [*inspect.signature(fake(folder)).parameters] == [*real.parameters]


def test_play_episode_fake_serves_the_traced_seed_7_episode():
    world = fake("worlds.make")("snake")
    rule = fake("baselines.rule_for")("snake", "default", "snake")
    episode = fake("core.play_episode")(world, {"snake": rule}, 7, trace=True)
    assert episode.result.seed == 7
    assert len(episode.frames) == episode.result.length + 1
    assert fake("core.play_episode")(world, {"snake": rule}, 7).frames is None
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="snake"):
        fake("core.play_episode")(world, {}, 7)


def test_make_fake_serves_the_snake_double_and_names_what_is_unknown():
    world = fake("worlds.make")("snake")
    assert world.spec == snake_spec()
    assert world.reset(42).obs.shape == (1, 11)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="'chess'"):
        fake("worlds.make")("chess")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="'many'"):
        fake("worlds.make")("snake", "many")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="grid"):
        fake("worlds.make")("snake", grid=12)


def test_rule_for_fake_serves_the_snake_rule_and_names_what_has_none():
    rule = fake("baselines.rule_for")("snake", "default", "snake")
    assert rule.description.startswith("Turn toward the food")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="fish"):
        fake("baselines.rule_for")("fish", "one", "fish")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="predator"):
        fake("baselines.rule_for")("snake", "default", "predator")


def test_baseline_means_fake_serves_the_snake_means():
    means = fake("baselines.baseline_means")("snake", "default")
    assert support.schema_errors(as_json(means), "baselines.BaselineMeans") == []
    assert set(means.random) == set(means.rule) == {"snake"}
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        fake("baselines.baseline_means")("prey", "default")


def test_make_learner_fake_serves_the_dqn_double_and_rejects_a_mismatch():
    dqn = support.method_config(support.read("methods.MethodConfig", "snake_dqn_defaults"))
    learner = fake("methods.make_learner")(dqn, snake_spec(), "snake", np.random.default_rng(1))
    assert learner.state_dict()["steps_seen"] == 10
    box_spec = replace(snake_spec(), action=Box(2, -1.0, 1.0))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"dqn.*Box"):
        fake("methods.make_learner")(dqn, box_spec, "snake", np.random.default_rng(1))
    ppo = support.method_config(support.read("methods.MethodConfig", "ppo_lr_override"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"ppo.*Discrete"):
        fake("methods.make_learner")(ppo, snake_spec(), "snake", np.random.default_rng(1))


def test_load_policy_fake_serves_the_rule_and_names_spec_mismatches():
    ckpt = support.checkpoint(support.read("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100pct"))
    policy = fake("methods.load_policy")(ckpt, "snake", snake_spec())
    obs, action = policy.table[0]
    assert np.array_equal(policy.act(obs, np.random.default_rng(0)), action)
    changed = replace(snake_spec(), obs_len=14, action=Box(2, -1.0, 1.0))
    with pytest.raises(SpecMismatchError) as caught:
        fake("methods.load_policy")(ckpt, "snake", changed)
    assert set(caught.value.fields) == {"obs_len", "action"}
    assert "obs_len (11 != 14)" in str(caught.value)


def test_load_run_configs_fake_serves_seeds_1_and_2():
    configs = fake("train.load_run_configs")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), [2, 1])
    assert [c.name for c in configs] == ["snake-default-s2", "snake-default-s1"]
    assert all(c.methods["snake"].settings["lr"] == 0.0005 for c in configs)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        fake("train.load_run_configs")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), [3])


def test_run_check_fake_serves_the_passing_dqn_check():
    [result] = fake("train.run_check")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"))
    assert result.passed
    assert result.method == "dqn"
    assert result.agreement >= 0.95


def test_run_train_fake_writes_the_run_folder(tmp_path):
    config = replace(snake_config(), name="dup", budget=2000)
    record = fake("train.run_train")(config, tmp_path)
    folder = tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / "dup"
    assert record.path == folder
    assert record.config == config
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert json.loads((folder / "run.json").read_text())["config"]["name"] == "dup"
    assert len((folder / "evals.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()) == 21
    assert sorted(p.name for p in (folder / "checkpoints").iterdir()) == [
        "ckpt_0.pt",
        "ckpt_100.pt",
        "ckpt_50.pt",
    ]
    saved = torch.load(folder / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_50.pt", weights_only=True)
    assert saved["step"] == 1000
    before = (folder / "run.json").read_bytes()
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError) as caught:
        fake("train.run_train")(config, tmp_path)
    assert caught.value.path == folder
    assert (folder / "run.json").read_bytes() == before


def test_run_batch_fake_serves_two_finished_records():
    records = fake("train.run_batch")(Path("configs/snake-smoke.toml"), [1, 2], Path("runs"))
    assert [r.config.name for r in records] == ["snake-smoke-s1", "snake-smoke-s2"]
    assert {r.status for r in records} == {"finished"}


def test_find_runs_fake_serves_finished_runs_plus_named_ones(tmp_path):
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs")(tmp_path, "snake", "default")
    assert [r.record.config.seed for r in runs] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    assert {r.record.status for r in runs} == {"finished"}
    named = fake("runlog.find_runs")(tmp_path, "snake", "default", include=["snake-default-s6"])
    assert named[-1].record.status == "incomplete"
    assert named[-1].record.path == tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / "snake-default-s6"
    assert fake("runlog.find_runs")(tmp_path, "fish", "one") == []


def test_open_run_fake_serves_the_run_double_at_the_path(tmp_path):
    run = fake("runlog.open_run")(tmp_path / "snake-default-s1")
    assert run.record.path == tmp_path / "snake-default-s1"
    assert len(run.checkpoints()) == 3


def test_progress_fake_serves_the_35pct_progress():
    snapshot = fake("runlog.progress")(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"))
    assert snapshot.step / snapshot.budget == pytest.approx(0.35)
    assert snapshot.latest.step == snapshot.step
    assert snapshot.elapsed.total_seconds() == 1260.0


def test_learning_curve_fake_writes_a_png_named_after_the_runs(tmp_path):
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs")(tmp_path, "snake", "default")
    means = fake("baselines.baseline_means")("snake", "default")
    output = fake("charts.learning_curve")(runs, means, tmp_path / "reports")
    assert output.kind == "chart"
    assert output.path.read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG")
    assert output.made_from == tuple(f"snake-default-s{i}" for i in range(1, 6))
    assert support.schema_errors(as_json(output), "core.Output") == []


def test_final_evaluation_fake_serves_the_passing_summary(tmp_path):
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs")(tmp_path, "snake", "default")
    [summary] = fake("evaluate.final_evaluation")(runs, tmp_path)
    assert summary.verdict.beats_baselines
    assert all(c.rejected for c in summary.comparisons)
    assert support.schema_errors(as_json(summary), "evaluate.Summary") == []

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a8b65a52fd63d455a/tests/contracts/test_cli.py
"""`cli.main` dispatches each M1 subcommand to its pipeline entry and prints its result."""

from pathlib import Path

import contract_support as support
import pytest

from rllab.cli import main as main_module
from rllab.cli.main import main, seed_list


def record_calls(monkeypatch, entry, result):
    calls = []

    def fake_entry(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append((args, kwargs))
        return result

    monkeypatch.setattr(main_module, entry, fake_entry)
    return calls


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("argv", "entry", "args", "kwargs"),
    [
        (["check", "c.toml"], "run_check", (Path("c.toml"), 0), {}),
        (["check", "c.toml", "--seed", "3"], "run_check", (Path("c.toml"), 3), {}),
        (["train", "c.toml", "--seed", "2"], "run_batch", (Path("c.toml"), [2], Path("runs")), {}),
        (
            ["batch", "c.toml", "--seeds", "1-3", "--root", "out", "--workers", "2"],
            "run_batch",
            (Path("c.toml"), [1, 2, 3], Path("out")),
            {"workers": 2},
        ),
        (["watch", "runs/snake/default/a"], "run_watch", (Path("runs/snake/default/a"),), {}),
        (
            ["chart", "snake", "default", "--include", "a", "--measure", "length"],
            "run_chart",
            (Path("runs"), "snake", "default"),
            {"include": ["a"], "measure": "length"},
        ),
        (
            ["evaluate", "snake", "default", "--out", "e"],
            "run_evaluate",
            (Path("runs"), "snake", "default", Path("e")),
            {"include": []},
        ),
    ],
)
def test_each_subcommand_calls_its_entry(monkeypatch, capsys, argv, entry, args, kwargs):
    calls = record_calls(monkeypatch, entry, ["first", "second"])
    assert main(argv) == 0
    assert calls == [(args, kwargs)]
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "first\nsecond\n"


def test_evaluate_writes_under_the_dated_eval_folder_by_default(monkeypatch):
    calls = record_calls(monkeypatch, "run_evaluate", [])
    assert main(["evaluate", "snake", "default"]) == 0
    out = calls[0][0][3]
    assert out.parent == Path("runs/snake/default/_eval")


def test_the_entry_result_is_printed(monkeypatch, capsys):
    snapshot = support.progress(support.read("runlog.Progress", "snake_35pct"))
    record_calls(monkeypatch, "run_watch", snapshot)
    assert main(["watch", "run"]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == f"{snapshot}\n"


def test_an_error_is_one_line_naming_its_cause(monkeypatch, capsys):
    def missing(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no run at {path}")

    monkeypatch.setattr(main_module, "run_watch", missing)
    assert main(["watch", "nowhere"]) == 1
    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    assert err == "rllab watch: FileNotFoundError: no run at nowhere\n"


def test_an_unknown_subcommand_is_a_usage_error():
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as caught:
        main(["fly"])
    assert caught.value.code == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("text", "seeds"), [("7", [7]), ("1-5", [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]), ("1,3-4", [1, 3, 4])])
def test_seed_lists(text, seeds):
    assert seed_list(text) == seeds

59	def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
60	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab", description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
61	    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
62	
63	    check = sub.add_parser("check", help="train each team method on its check world")
64	    check.add_argument("config", type=Path)
65	    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
66	    check.set_defaults(run=_check)
67	
68	    train = sub.add_parser("train", help="one training run: a one-seed batch")
69	    train.add_argument("config", type=Path)
70	    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
71	    train.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=RUNS)
72	    train.set_defaults(run=_train)

74	    batch = sub.add_parser("batch", help="the method check, then one run per seed")

def _add_run_selection(command: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
    """The runs of one world and variant under `--root`, plus unfinished ones `--include`d."""
    command.add_argument("world")
    command.add_argument("variant")
    command.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME")
    command.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=RUNS)
